# Graph Report - pr1313  (2026-08-21)

## Corpus Check
- 1444 files · ~2,078,940 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 28770 nodes · 53728 edges · 1182 communities (1074 shown, 108 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 95% EXTRACTED · 5% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 2639 edges (avg confidence: 0.91)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `440f78e8`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
